After they exchanged such light greetings, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon casually mentioned Geom Mugeuk.

“It may not be going as you intend, Cult Leader, but the Second Young Master has been distinguishing himself within the cult lately.”

“Is that not your work?”

In truth, it was fair to say so, since Geom Mugeuk had gained popularity while becoming entangled with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“The Second Young Master is your work, Cult Leader. I only realized this time what a splendid work he is.”

Geom Woojin did not deny it. Believing that he existed behind his son’s accomplishments was not a bad thing, in any sense.

“He is still immature.”

“I sometimes think back to when I was the Second Young Master’s age. If he is immature, then I should have been called vermin.”

“You overestimate immaturity too much.”

“You were the first to overestimate him, Cult Leader.”

He was referring to the decision to entrust Geom Mugeuk with the post of Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion. Whatever reason one gave, it had been an extraordinary appointment.

“And so, that is why I say this: I would like to make the Second Young Master into an even better work.”

“If you step forward, it could be called a fortuitous encounter for my second son.”

“You praise me too highly.”

“Do as you see fit.”

“Thank you.”

At the end of the conversation, the purpose of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s visit emerged cautiously.

“Since you have given your permission, will you support him properly?”

“What do you mean?”

“As for this matter of the Demon Army Lord, please allow it to proceed according to my wishes.”

It was a request for Jang Ho to be made the Demon Army Lord, as he had recommended.

A brief silence passed. Without any fluctuation of emotion, the two men looked at each other. The one who broke the silence first was Geom Woojin.

“The matter of the Demon Army Lord is difficult.”

“This matter is also what the Second Young Master desires.”

“As you said yourself, I have supported my second son enough. I must consider my eldest as well.”

“There is one thing I have learned while raising disciples. When you support someone, you must support one person decisively. Because I failed to do that, I am cursed by many.”

“I will be fine. Unlike the tolerant you, I kill everyone who curses me.”

Their gazes tangled in midair. They shared years long enough to understand each other’s intent from a mere look. The longer those years were, the more awkward the silence of this moment became.

“Do you have anything more to say?”

At the Heavenly Demon’s order of dismissal, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not insist any further. He knew well that once the man made a decision, he was not one to change it easily. Another method was needed.

“I will see you again another time.”

“Do that.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon turned and left. His expression remained stiff the entire way as he walked back along the path of blood.

‘What is the meaning of weighing things on a scale so belatedly? If you chose to support him once, you should support him to the end. Tsk!’

* * *

“The Cult Leader seemed to be thinking more of the Eldest Young Master than of you.”

The moment the Blood Heaven Blade Demon saw me, he began trying to drive a wedge between my father and me.

“He seemed to be considering the Eldest Young Master as his successor.”

When one has a weakness, one shakes the other party first instead. This old man’s political maneuvering was truly hateful.

“It seems ours was an ill-fated meeting.”

“What do you mean?”

“If I am being pushed back by my elder brother, then you, at least, should fill in what I lack…”

“Are you saying I am lacking?”

“I am saying it is somewhat regrettable.”

Instead of getting angry, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon tried to soothe me.

“Look here, Second Young Master. Making Jang Ho the Demon Army Lord was impossible from the start.”

“Have you already given up?”

“There is no need to cling to something that cannot be done.”

“You acted so high and mighty when you cannot even manage this much?”

“This much? High and mighty?”

I began provoking the indignant Blood Heaven Blade Demon even further.

“You were the one who proposed becoming my backing.”

“And you were the one who whined like a child for me to do the impossible.”

“Then you should have refused back then. You should not have recommended Jang Ho the next day. Did you not think there might be a chance?”

I drew a long line beneath the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s feet.

“You said this before, did you not? That this was the extent of the Cult Leader’s regard for you. I believed in this line…”

I drew another line, not even halfway as long.

“Was the actual line between my father and you not here?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon could not refute it. What would have happened if I had not gone to my father and asked separately? Would Father truly have granted the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s request?

“If Father refused, you should have asked again. If it had been me, I would have accomplished it without fail.”

“Then try it.”

“What?”

“Did you not mock me, saying I could not even manage this much? Then you should be able to do it easily.”

“What will you do if I ask Father and accomplish it with ease?”

“Go ahead and try. It will probably only backfire.”

He seemed certain that because his own request had been refused, there was no way the request of a son would be accepted. At times like this, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon knew my father well.

“Are you suggesting a wager?”

“A wager? Good. Let us make one. If you fail as well, then from that moment on, become my water without complaint. And no more whining about side dishes.”

“And if I succeed?”

“I will grant what you want.”

“Whatever I want?”

“Is there something you want?”

After thinking for a moment, I said to him,

“Obtain a spiritual medicine for me. One at least better than the Demon Essence Pill.”

For an instant, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon flinched in surprise. I knew exactly the nature of that surprise.

“Do you know how difficult it is to obtain a spiritual medicine better than the Demon Essence Pill?”

“I know, which is why I am making it a condition. Since we have decided to ride the same boat, does my becoming stronger not mean that you become stronger as well?”

There was a reason I demanded a spiritual medicine from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

I knew he had one.

He possessed the Heavenly Beyond Divine Pill, a peerless spiritual medicine that could grant more internal energy than the Demon Essence Pill.

In the past, when the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had fully joined hands with my elder brother, he had given him the Heavenly Beyond Divine Pill as a gift. It was revealed then that he had kept it without taking it himself for many long years.

The reason I had led him into this wager was precisely because of that Heavenly Beyond Divine Pill.

“You cannot make Jang Ho the Demon Army Lord, nor can you obtain a spiritual medicine for me. I do not see why I should join hands with you.”

“Fine. If you put Jang Ho in the seat of Demon Army Lord, I will obtain a spiritual medicine for you.”

“You promise?”

I drew the Black Demon Sword and held it forward. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon struck my sword with the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber as if flicking it away.

Clang!

That clear sound was our promise. A promise made with one’s unique weapon held a far stronger binding force than words.

As always, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon sprang up with a swift motion and vanished into the distance.

If I won the wager, he would keep his promise. The Heavenly Beyond Divine Pill was something he had prepared for his successor.

If I could take the Heavenly Beyond Divine Pill after having already taken the Demon Essence Pill, then I would not lose a fight against a Demon Venerable because I lacked internal energy.

And I knew how to win this wager.

* * *

The place I went to was the office of Grand Strategist Sama Myeong.

This Tongcheon Pavilion, where he resided, served as the head of our cult. All information from the martial world gathered here and was sorted. Among it, only an extremely small portion passed through Sama Myeong and reached my father.

I could not know for certain, but a considerable portion of the information being sent from Tongcheon Pavilion to the Heavenly Demon Hall these days was probably about me.

From the entrance of Tongcheon Pavilion to Sama Myeong’s office, strict security was spread out, even more stringent than at the Heavenly Demon Hall. That was proof of how important a role Tongcheon Pavilion played in our cult, and since it was a place where strategists who used their heads gathered, it needed to be protected even more thoroughly.

When I entered the Chief Strategist’s office, Sama Myeong was working amid piles of documents. There were so many documents stacked on his desk that they nearly hid his face.

One might think he only needed to offer a few words of advice by Father’s side, but in reality, the work of the Chief Strategist was a battle against documents. Dispatches flew in from all over the Central Plains by the hundreds each day, and he spent the whole day reading and rereading them, distinguishing information to discard from information to preserve.

Knowing that well, I could serve as the Pavilion Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion, but not as the Lord of Tongcheon Pavilion.

Sama Myeong rose from his desk and welcomed me gladly.

“What brings you here? Come, please sit over here.”

I sat across from him at the table. Soon after, as if waiting, a subordinate brought tea.

“I do not know if it will suit your taste. We only have one kind of tea here.”

“The fragrance is excellent.”

As I took a sip of tea, I looked around. Befitting the workplace of the greatest intellect in our cult, all sorts of books filled the shelves on every side.

“I hear that whenever the warriors of our cult gather these days, all they talk about is Hwangcheon Pavilion.”

“Are they not too busy cursing it?”

“Surely not. I heard it is nothing but praise.”

I knew public opinion about Hwangcheon Pavilion better than he did. As many people were thrilled by the idea of enforcing the law fairly, there were also those who were beginning to resist it.

Since they had lived as they pleased until now, they must have felt needless anxiety. What if I get dragged away too? That sort of worry, surely. Before that atmosphere spread, discipline had to be firmly established.

“But what brings you here?”

“There is something I would like to ask you, Strategist.”

“Please ask.”

“What do you think of Jang Ho?”

“I think he is a good warrior. He is a man of fine caliber who can lead the Demon Army well.”

Sama Myeong likely already knew. That I was behind this recommendation. That I was trying to seat Jang Ho in the position of Demon Army Lord.

“Then what are the chances that Jang Ho will rise to the position of Demon Army Lord?”

“To speak honestly, not even ten percent.”

“Even though the Blade Demon recommended Jang Ho?”

“The Cult Leader refused that recommendation.”

That was likely why he had given a figure of ten percent. Originally, his answer would have been impossible, but out of courtesy, it had become ten percent.

“What if Father changes his mind?”

“Then of course it would be possible. But it will not be easy. As you know, the Cult Leader is not someone who changes a decision he has made without any justification.”

“I know that well.”

To begin with, it was a decision made because of my request. I had no intention of asking such a father to change his decision again without any justification.

I had to create a justification for him to change it. This was what I had prepared.

“What about this? What if, following the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, we receive the recommendation of another Demon Venerable as well?”

“Two Demon Venerables recommending the same person at the same time? There is no precedent for it… but if that happens, then there would certainly be a sufficient possibility of Jang Ho becoming the Demon Army Lord. The Cult Leader would have justification to change his mind as well. But would that be possible?”

With the Blood Heaven Blade Demon having stepped forward, making another Demon Venerable step forward and recommend Jang Ho would never be easy.

“I will have to try. Sometimes, dealing with two tigers at once may be easier than dealing with one, no?”

“I think it would be more difficult.”

That was why this prerequisite had to exist. To avoid the unfortunate outcome of having my arms and legs torn off at the same time.

“What if those two tigers hated each other bitterly?”

Chapter 35: Better Not Forget.
------------------------------
“It’s unjust. I swear I didn’t do it!”

The person pleading his innocence in the interrogation room of the Underworld Pavilion was Bae Jongtak (裵宗鐸), a martial artist of the Northern Heaven Sword Family led by the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

He had been arrested after being accused of assaulting one of the family’s young maids. In the past, the incident would never even have become a case, but this time Bae Jongtak was arrested immediately on the day after the accusation was made.

“That maid is framing me! Let me confront her! I’m innocent.”

“Why would she frame you?”

“How should I know? Perhaps someone put her up to it, or maybe I made some sort of mistake with her. But at the very least, I did not assault her.”

He truly appeared to be wronged.

“Fine. I’ll let you confront her.”

“Bring her here at once.”

At my signal, Seo Daeryong, who had been waiting outside, brought in a girl who looked about thirteen or fourteen years old. The child was utterly terrified.

“Hey! Why the hell are you doing this to me?”

Bae Jongtak suddenly shouted at the girl.

I raised a hand to calm him down.

“Quiet! If you raise your voice one more time, there will be no confrontation.”

“Yes. I only did that because I’m being treated unfairly.”

I asked the girl,

“Is he the man who assaulted you?”

The girl slowly nodded.

“See that? She nodded without even properly looking at my face.”

“Why would she need to look closely at the face of someone she knows?”

“She can’t look at me because she feels guilty.”

“Are you really saying it wasn’t you?”

“No. I swear to heaven it wasn’t me. If you punish me merely because you believe the word of a child, I’ll be terribly wronged. Think about it. If I had been craving a woman, I could have gone to a brothel. Why would I touch a child that young?”

“How would I know? You tell me. Do you get excited when you see children?”

“Didn’t I say it wasn’t me?”

Bae Jongtak glared menacingly at the girl.

“You don’t know how frightening it is to be charged with making a false accusation, do you? I’ll forgive you, so tell the truth. Say it wasn’t me!”

The girl shrank back as if terrified.

“Look at her! She’s trembling!”

“That’s because she’s afraid. Didn’t you threaten to find and kill even her family back in her hometown if she revealed that you assaulted her?”

This case had not been reported by the girl herself. She had told another maid, who had gone to report it at the Underworld Pavilion branch established across from the Pleasure House on her friend’s behalf.

“Why do you only believe that girl?”

“Because I believe her.”

“Isn’t that terribly biased?”

“Because it’s certain that you assaulted her.”

“Then show me definitive evidence! Don’t just believe that girl’s word.”

When I looked at Seo Daeryong, he went outside. He returned with two more girls. They were slightly older than the girl who had entered earlier.

The moment Bae Jongtak saw them, he flinched.

“You remember them, don’t you? They used to be your maids.”

“Yes, I remember them. You’ve grown so much!”

Bae Jongtak greeted them warmly, but the girls only lowered their heads without answering.

“While listening to the girl’s testimony, I suddenly became curious. If you committed the crime this time, could it be that you hadn’t done the same before?”

After searching for his former maids and investigating, we discovered that they too had been assaulted.

“I’ve already heard all their testimonies. The way you assaulted the girls was identical. So were the threats. You toyed with them for two or three years, then replaced them with another girl.”

Once he realized it was no longer possible to deny everything, Bae Jongtak’s expression changed. The man who had been pleading his innocence disappeared, revealing his true nature.

“I made a mistake while drunk. Please let me off just this once.”

His sudden composure was so different from the man who had been insisting on his innocence a moment ago that it felt like watching the performance of a troupe of street entertainers.

He spoke calmly to the girls.

“Honestly, you enjoyed it too, didn’t you? I even gave you money every time we finished.”

At that, the youngest maid took coins from her clothing and threw them onto the floor. The coins spilled out with a clatter. She had kept every single one without spending a penny and brought them here today.

Bae Jongtak turned his head as if he had not seen it and pleaded with me.

“I heard that in the history of the Underworld Pavilion, no one has ever been sent to the prison for laying hands on a maid.”

“You did your homework in advance. That’s right.”

“I’ll never do such a thing again. I swear it to heaven. If you want me to write a pledge, I’ll do that too. No—in fact, I won’t keep any female maids at all.”

“You’re not going to prison.”

At my words, the girls raised their heads. Every one of them wore a terrified expression.

“Thank you. Thank you so much.”

The man grinned and looked at the girls, who all avoided his gaze and lowered their heads.

“But you do have to go somewhere else.”

“Where?”

“Here.”

Whoosh! Thud!

With a single stroke, my sword severed his neck. Without even managing a scream, he died instantly and toppled backward together with the chair.

Startled by the sudden turn of events, the girls cried out. His blood flowed over the coins scattered across the floor.

I immediately turned toward the girls.

“The man who tormented you is dead now. Your families have nothing to fear either. So from now on, live without worrying.”

The girls stared at me in shock.

“You will never forget what happened to you, not until the day you die. If you cannot forget it anyway, then don’t forget what happened today. Cover everything you suffered with this moment, when that man paid for his crimes. Remember only this final end of his.”

This was the best I could do for the girls.

The girls looked at the corpse, then turned to one another.

After a moment, the oldest among them stepped forward and bowed respectfully.

“Thank you so much for avenging us.”

The other girls bowed together with her.

“Thank you, sir. Sob…”

The youngest maid burst into tears, and another girl embraced her. Tears also streamed down the eyes of the girl who held her.

No one cared about how maids were treated. I had been the same way in the past.

“You don’t need to thank me. I was able to punish that man because you bravely testified. This was your retribution. So continue to live bravely and well from now on.”

“Yes.”

“And there is absolutely no chance that his friends or family will retaliate against you. From now on, the Underworld Pavilion will protect you.”

Now even the oldest girl began to shed tears.

“Thank you… truly.”

Father, if we fail to protect these children because we are blinded by the so-called law of the strong prevailing, then our heads will be severed by those orthodox sect bastards you hate so much. No—we deserve to have them severed. We ought to have no excuse even if our heads are cut off.

The investigators came in and took the girls away. I had instructed them to pay particular attention to the children from then on, so the girls had nothing to worry about.

Word of this would spread throughout the school as well, which would reduce the number of fools who dared lay hands on maids. We had to correct the very misconception that such treatment was only natural because they were demonic practitioners.

Ian, who had been watching the investigation, shook his head.

“Until I saw that man denying everything earlier, I really thought he had been wronged. Humans are truly frightening.”

“Humans are more frightening than ghosts.”

“But he belongs to the Northern Heaven Sword Family. Will the Ilhwa Sword Venerable really just let this pass?”

“His subordinate had been repeatedly assaulting young maids. Would he really make an issue of it because we killed someone like that? It would be better if he didn’t.”

“Aren’t you afraid, Young Master?”

“Of course I am. I’m afraid I’ll die before I can clear out all these pieces of trash.”

“Ever since you became the Pavilion Master of the Underworld Pavilion, our school has been growing kinder and kinder. What if it ends up filled with people more virtuous than the Martial Alliance?”

I immediately corrected her joke.

“Not assaulting maids isn’t kindness, is it? It’s the bare minimum.”

“Ah… right.”

“We simply haven’t even been able to do that bare minimum until now.”

Just then, Seo Daeryong returned.

“Pavilion Master, why did you do that?”

“Do what?”

“You deliberately killed him, didn’t you? Your belief has always been that people like him should be locked in the prison and made to suffer rather than killed.”

The prison of our school was a place more painful than death itself.

“That’s right.”

“Then why did you kill him?”

As expected, Seo Daeryong was a perceptive man. I had deliberately handled a case connected to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable myself, and, just as he said, I had intentionally killed the man.

“Killing him this way gives me an official pretext to go meet the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.”

Seo Daeryong was startled.

“The Blood Heaven Demon Butcher is already enough of a headache. Why are you trying to meet the Ilhwa Sword Venerable as well?”

“Because I need a second tiger.”

“What?”

Seeing that Seo Daeryong had no idea what I meant, I gave him an order.

“Send word to the Sword Venerable immediately. Tell him that a problem arose during the interrogation, and that I will personally meet him to explain.”

* * *

An hour later, I headed toward the Northern Heaven Sword Family with Seo Daeryong.

“Do you really have to take me with you?”

“This is an official visit. Can the Pavilion Master go alone? His right-hand man has to accompany him.”

“When did I become your right-hand man?”

“A power-oriented, cold-hearted man, yet a true romantic who loves only one woman; a rebel who alone raises his hand when everyone else remains silent. You’re more than qualified to be my right-hand man.”

“Good heavens!”

“Which description did you like?”

“I didn’t like any of them!”

Before long, we arrived at the entrance to the Northern Heaven Sword Family.

If the Blood Heaven Demon Butcher’s Southern Way Sect occupied the south of the school, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s Northern Heaven Sword Family held the north.

“This is my first time visiting the Northern Heaven Sword Family. None of the cases I’ve handled so far had anything to do with them. Unlike the Blood Heaven Demon Butcher, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable has done a good job managing his subordinates. The demonic swordsmen of the Sword Family also considered honor important.”

“Is that so?”

When I responded indifferently, Seo Daeryong asked,

“Do you disagree?”

“From what I’ve seen, demonic swordsmen and demonic blade fiends are all much the same. Do the eyes of people who value honor really look like that?”

The gazes of the demonic swordsmen guarding the entrance were far from friendly.

It seemed they already knew about Bae Jongtak’s death. Rather than thinking, He died because he committed a crime, their feelings were closer to, You killed a martial artist of our Northern Heaven Sword Family just because he touched a maid?

Of course, times being what they were, they dared not provoke me, and soon they all bowed respectfully.

Seo Daeryong spoke quietly to me.

“As a pacifist, I’ve been seeing that look rather often lately. I keep seeing it whenever I’m with someone.”

“I’ll add ‘pacifist’ to the list of titles describing you.”

“Ah…”

A martial artist who had come to guide us was waiting nearby.

“The Demon Venerable is waiting for you. My name is Sa Ujong (沙佑鐘).”

Sa Ujong was known as the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s right-hand man. He was one of the three strongest people in the Northern Heaven Sword Family, and his presence was indeed as sharp as a blade.

I knew this man well—not because of any personal connection or friendship, but because his end had been rather memorable.

“Good to meet you.”

“Please follow me.”

Sa Ujong led us farther inside. We passed the buildings where the demonic swordsmen lived and proceeded to the deepest part of the family grounds.

There stood a small thatched cottage.

This neat and tastefully arranged place was the residence of the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“The Pavilion Master of the Underworld Pavilion has arrived.”

At Sa Ujong’s announcement, the door of the cottage opened, and a woman dressed in white stepped outside.

“Welcome, Pavilion Master of the Sword.”

The soft-voiced woman was none other than the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, So Yeonrang (昭蓮郞).

Chapter 36: I Am Already Loyal
------------------------------
The One-Flower Sword Sovereign was a beautiful woman.

Outwardly she looked to be in her twenties, but in truth she was more than twice that age. Because of that, there was a saying in our cult.

The world may change, but the Sword Sovereign does not.

If anything, she seemed to grow younger with each passing year.

But if anyone was fooled by that delicate, willowy appearance and tried anything foolish, their tongue would be cut out on the spot and a cold iron sword would be driven into their heart. The red camellia engraved on the scabbard of her unique weapon, the One-Flower Sword, was as vivid as blood.

“It has been a long time, Elder.”

“I never grow used to being called Elder. How about calling me Senior?”

She treated me with courtesy. Not only me—she was famous for always speaking respectfully and never neglecting etiquette even toward lower-ranking warriors.

“When I consider your seniority, I cannot do that.”

“You respect someone simply because they are old? I do not think so. Respect should be given to those worthy of respect. Just as one should respect someone young if they are deserving of it.”

“Very well. I will follow your wishes, Senior. In return, please call me Second Young Master comfortably.”

“I shall.”

I introduced Seo Daeryong to her.

“This is the special investigator who looked into the incident. I brought him to tell you what happened.”

Seo Daeryong cupped his fist respectfully to the One-Flower Sword Sovereign.

“I am Special Investigator Seo Daeryong. I will report on this incident.”

At that, the One-Flower Sword Sovereign raised a hand to stop him.

“That is enough. If the Yellow Springs Pavilion judged him guilty, then he must be guilty. A man who violated a young maid deserves death, so our Sword House will not take issue with what happened during the investigation.”

I signaled Seo Daeryong with my eyes to withdraw. Seo Daeryong quietly left the thatched hut.

The One-Flower Sword Sovereign looked at me and smiled faintly. She had realized that I had come for another reason.

“I hear that winds of change are blowing through our cult these days thanks to your efforts, Second Young Master.”

“I am merely running wild without knowing my place.”

“If our cult can advance, how could that be called running wild?”

Among the Eight Demon Sovereigns, the One-Flower Sword Sovereign was said to be the one least suited to the Demonic Cult. This shabby thatched hut alone proved it. It was tastefully arranged, like the secluded dwelling of an aged master from a prestigious orthodox sect.

“You are doing admirable work.”

“It is only possible because you are serving as a steadfast pillar, Senior.”

The One-Flower Sword Sovereign shook her head.

“Do not gild this wrinkled old face for no reason.”

Those words were clearly intentional. Words meant to invite a response.

“What are you saying? Even now, Senior, you look as though you are in your twenties.”

“My twenties? What nonsense!”

She waved her hands in denial, but an irrepressible smile bloomed on her face.

“I am someone who is content to step back and comprehend the principles of martial learning.”

But she had lived a life quite different from those words.

After Father was killed by Hwa Mugi and our cult was forced to seal its gates, a full-fledged power struggle broke out within. Not only the Eight Demon Sovereigns, but also the renowned masters of our cult each fought over the seat of cult leader.

So who was the first cult leader?

Amazingly, it was the woman before my eyes—the One-Flower Sword Sovereign. It was hard to believe when looking at her gentle smile and calm demeanor now, but she had moved before anyone else and seized the seat of cult leader.

A storm of blood followed in the process. She was merciless. Throughout her brief tenure as cult leader, blood continued to flow. It would have been better if she had at least been skilled in politics, but the One-Flower Sword Sovereign’s ability to rule did not keep pace with her ambition.

She caused every tragedy that occurs when an incompetent person has too much greed, and before three years had passed, she lost her life to the next cult leader. That was why my assessment of her could only be harsh.

“To be honest, when this incident broke out, I felt greatly burdened.”

“Why did you feel burdened?”

“As you may have heard, there have been many clashes with the Namdo Sect recently.”

I subtly mentioned the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s Namdo Sect. Among the Eight Demon Sovereigns, those two were known to have the worst relationship. For that reason, the Blade Ghosts and the Demon Swords were likewise like dogs and monkeys.

“Elder Blood Heaven Blade Demon pressured me severely. And now, on top of that, conflict has arisen with the Sword House…”

“I am clearly a different person from the Blade Demon!”

As if even being mentioned together with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon displeased her, the One-Flower Sword Sovereign’s voice rose.

“Of course. Naturally, you are different.”

“Since the subject has come up, I will ask. I heard that this time, the Blade Demon recommended a man named Jang Ho from the Namdo Sect as Demon Army Lord. What happened?”

Though she seemed as if she would do nothing but train alone in this hut, she was aware of everything happening within the cult. As if to prove that her future seizure of power had not been mere chance.

“Jang Ho is exceptionally capable and loyal.”

“What I am asking is why the Blade Demon tried to make him Demon Army Lord.”

“To be honest, Jang Ho is my man.”

As if she had already known, the One-Flower Sword Sovereign was not surprised. This was what she had been curious about.

“How did you persuade the Blade Demon?”

“I provoked his pride. I told him that as a Demon Sovereign in name and fact, should he not make a choice for the cult? That he should recommend someone with true ability, not because of personal ties or political power.”

“The Blade Demon would not be persuaded by such reasons.”

“That is true. It was probably because he did not want to let me slip away. In any case, the Blade Demon said this: that among the Eight Demon Sovereigns, his heart for the cult was the greatest.”

The One-Flower Sword Sovereign’s expression crumpled as if she found the words absurd, but I pretended not to notice and continued speaking.

“However, it seems that even for him, seating Jang Ho as Demon Army Lord will not be easy. The Chief Strategist proposed a method, but that too is difficult.”

“What method did the Strategist mention?”

“He said it would be possible if another Demon Sovereign recommended him as well. The problem is that there is no Demon Sovereign who would do that with him.”

“Why do you think there is none?”

“Pardon?”

“Do you think the Blade Demon is the only Demon Sovereign who cares for the cult?”

“That is not what I meant.”

The One-Flower Sword Sovereign asked me in a cold gaze.

“Is your purpose in coming to see me today not to prod even my pride and ask me to recommend Jang Ho together with Senior Blade Demon?”

Now was the time to show honesty. When stratagem and truth were mixed together so thoroughly that one could no longer tell what ingredients made up the scheme, only then did a scheme gain its flavor.

“You noticed?”

“Why are you going this far?”

“To get the Demon Army into my hands. With that influence, I wish to become successor as quickly as possible.”

The One-Flower Sword Sovereign silently stared at me.

Why had that fastidious old man, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, chosen me? Even going so far as to abandon my elder brother. She was surely harboring such doubts.

“Now I understand clearly why the Cult Leader seated you as Master of the Yellow Springs Pavilion.”

“Why? I still do not understand my father’s intentions.”

She came closer and stared into my eyes as though peering into them.

“Second Young Master, you have a strange way of shaking people’s hearts.”

As if only someone overflowing with confidence could reveal such candid emotion, she laid bare exactly what she was feeling.

“I will take that as praise.”

After pondering for a moment, she finally made her decision.

“Very well. I will also recommend Jang Ho.”

“Truly?”

I put on a startled expression, but inwardly, I had expected to some degree that she would recommend him. Precisely because of her special relationship with the Blade Demon.

“May I ask the reason?”

“Let us say it is to show that the Blade Demon is not the only Demon Sovereign who cares for the cult.”

She turned away. Knowing it was a silent order to leave, I bowed to her respectfully.

“I will not forget this kindness.”

She gave no answer, but I knew exactly why.

The reason she was stepping forward in this matter was entirely because of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Because there was the possibility that I was someone fit to become the successor, and that the Blade Demon had recognized it first. If so, she did not want to let the Blade Demon monopolize me. Because she would not want to give the Blade Demon even horse dung lying by the roadside.

Why?

Because she truly, terribly hated the Blade Demon.

When she became cult leader, the very first person she killed was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

* * *

The next day, the One-Flower Sword Sovereign recommended Jang Ho as Demon Army Lord.

The cult was thrown into an uproar at this sudden news. Never before had a captain from the Demon Army become a candidate, and never before had two Demon Sovereigns joined their wills to recommend one person.

Especially since those two Demon Sovereigns were the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One-Flower Sword Sovereign, who did not get along—how could it not be shocking? Naturally, Jang Ho became the center of attention.

In the Heavenly Demon Hall as well, Cult Leader Geom Ujin and Chief Strategist Sama Myeong were discussing the matter.

“I never imagined Second Young Master would draw in the Sword Sovereign.”

“He must have used the discord between the two.”

“Everyone knows of their discord, but no one could have accomplished this matter so easily.”

After all, neither the Blood Heaven Blade Demon nor the One-Flower Sword Sovereign was easy to deal with. If one tried to use a feud that everyone plainly knew about, it would only backfire.

“What of this man Jang Ho? Can the Demon Army be entrusted to him?”

“In terms of ability alone, he is outstanding. However, he is close to a pure martial artist, so his political footing is weak. Choosing Captain Jang in itself was a very clever choice.”

“He likely did not discover Jang Ho from the beginning.”

“What do you mean?”

“It must have been an emotional choice. He is a more emotional child than one might think.”

Sama Myeong found Geom Ujin’s words unfamiliar. Until now, he had never spoken to him of his assessment of his child.

“That is both a strength and a weakness.”

At Sama Myeong’s words, Geom Ujin spoke firmly.

“It is a weakness.”

Sama Myeong held back his words with a faint smile. Recently, not only he but everyone could feel that the Cult Leader was opening his heart to Geom Mugeuk.

But even so, he did not think the Cult Leader would make Geom Mugeuk his successor. Geom Ujin often made decisions entirely different from what he showed outwardly. It was still too early to know.

“How shall we conclude the matter of the Demon Army Lord?”

“If the two of them recommended him, we must accept it. Their dignity must be considered.”

As if he had been waiting, Geom Ujin issued an order.

“Seat Jang Ho as Demon Army Lord.”

* * *

“I do not know how I can ever repay this grace.”

Upon hearing that he had become Demon Army Lord, Jang Ho came to see me first. He felt both joy and worry at the fact that he had become Demon Army Lord.

“Given your personality, Captain Jang, you probably will not be able to repay it.”

“Pardon? What do you mean by that?”

“There are two reasons I seated you as Demon Army Lord. The first is because I thought you were the person best suited for the position. Not for your sake or mine, but for the sake of the Demon Army and our cult, I judged you to be the right person.”

“I am overwhelmed that you regard me so highly.”

“The second reason is because I hope the Demon Army will become my strength. But do the first and second reasons not conflict? It will not be easy for someone as upright as you to maintain a private relationship. Ah, and let me say this in advance: I have not the slightest intention of forcing friendship upon you, so do not worry.”

At that, Jang Ho said something unexpected.

“Do you remember when you first met me? I submitted an anonymous petition because of my friend’s death. It was for revenge. I even staked my life on it. As you can see, I am an emotional person who values relationships with people above all else in life. If you saw me as someone who would discard private relationships for the sake of the organization, then you saw me wrong. If you had not come then, Young Master, I probably would have died at that time.”

In truth, that was exactly what had happened. If I had not gone, he would have died because of that incident.

“I wish to be loyal to you, Young Master. No, I am already loyal to you. Of course I will be loyal to the Cult Leader and the cult, and I will strive for the Demon Army, but the person I am most loyal to is you, Young Master.”

He was speaking sincerely. I could tell. I had gained a far better subordinate than I had thought.

“I also wish to become someone like you, Young Master.”

“What kind of person am I?”

“Someone who makes it feel as though, if I am with you, I can accomplish even the most difficult things.”

“With that level of flattery, I suppose there is no need to worry you will suffer disadvantages for being bad at politics.”

“Haha, I am not that clean a man.”

“I look forward to working with you.”

“As do I.”

I firmly clasped his large hand. I liked the unexpectedness of this big, rough man.

Chapter 37: What Lies Beyond the Door.
--------------------------------------
“Training ends today.”

At Jang Ho’s words, the enforcement martial artists showed their regret. It had been training so brutal they could no longer count how many times they had cursed, but they had gained much from it.

Their martial arts had not advanced by leaps and bounds, but at the very least, their slackened minds had been pulled taut again.

For experts on the level of enforcement martial artists, a difference in mental strength could be said to lead directly to a difference in skill.

On top of that, Jang Ho had taken time here and there to look over their martial arts, correcting improper postures and bad habits. Thanks to that, quite a few had seen genuine improvement in their martial skill.

Until the very end, Jang Ho was telling each enforcement martial artist how to correct their stance and what sort of training they should do from now on.

Truly, none of the enforcement martial artists who had undergone that hellish training could have imagined they would say such a thing.

“Could you not train us a little longer?”

That was when I, who had been watching from the side, stepped forward.

“That will be difficult. Captain Jang has ascended to the position of Demon Army Lord.”

At the words Demon Army Lord, everyone was startled. They had been confined to the training ground and had not heard any news from outside.

“Congratulations.”

At someone’s congratulations, cheers and applause burst out.

Jang Ho left them with his final farewell.

“I will never forget the time I spent with you. Then, I’ll see you next time. No, wait. From my position, if I end up seeing you all, that means something terrible has happened. Let’s never see each other again as long as we live!”

After offering that final farewell, even mixing in a joke, Jang Ho left the place.

I stood on the platform where Jang Ho had been standing. The enforcement martial artists’ gazes were quite different from what they had been on the first day of training.

“You there, the first and last martial artists ever to be trained by a Demon Army Lord!”

At my words, several of them burst into laughter.

“Now, even if I ordered you to go arrest my father, you could do it without fear, couldn’t you?”

Laughter broke out here and there again.

After loosening the atmosphere like that, I conveyed what I had wanted to say to them.

“Martial arts are important, and fighting well is important too. But I believe the most important thing for those who bear the name of enforcement martial artist is pride. Pride in upholding the rules of our cult and protecting its order. We walk our own path. Understood?”

“Yes!”

“You’ve all worked hard in training. I’ve prepared a banquet, so today, drink your fill and rest!”

Cheers of joy erupted even louder than their vigorous reply from before.

* * *

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was waiting for me in the place where we met every day.

“The sight of you standing tall here is like a guardian stone watching over me.”

“Are you mocking me by calling me a stone head?”

“How could I possibly?”

I had thought he would fly into a rage because I had dragged the One Flower Sword Venerable into this, but unlike usual, he was calm.

“Congratulations on seating Jang Ho as the Demon Army Lord.”

“Thank you.”

I was more tense than ever. I had no idea what this old man might suddenly do.

But today’s Blood Heaven Blade Demon, just as Jang Ho had done, was showing an unexpected side unlike his usual self.

He was extremely composed. So much so that I wondered if perhaps this man called the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had originally been this sort of person.

This was why one must not judge a person after seeing only one side of them. One must not be deceived by the front. I still did not know what the back side of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked like.

“How did you persuade the Sword Venerable?”

“If you promise not to get angry, I’ll tell you honestly.”

“I won’t get angry.”

“I pricked Senior Sword Venerable’s pride by saying that you were the true Demon Venerable. I used you, Elder.”

The corners of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s mouth curled upward.

“In that case, you were the one who was fooled. There’s no way that schemer wouldn’t have seen through your shallow ploy.”

He disliked the One Flower Sword Venerable, but at the very least, he did not underestimate her.

The same went for the Sword Venerable. The fact that she had intervened in this matter also meant she trusted the Blade Demon’s eye for people.

“That fox will definitely try to scheme against you. She’ll use the fact that she helped you as a reason to keep you from meeting me. What will you do then?”

“For me, you come first, Elder.”

“She’ll bring up the fact that she helped Jang Ho.”

“That’s fine, since you helped me first, Elder.”

“Will that still be the case when she makes you an offer you can’t refuse? Like this, for example.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon took a small box from his robe and held it out.

“A promise is a promise.”

I accepted the box and carefully opened it.

A single blue-tinged pill was emitting a mysterious spiritual energy, as if it were not of this world.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon spoke with an expression that showed it truly pained him to part with it.

“It is the Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill.”

At this moment, I had no need to pretend to be surprised. I really was surprised to see him hand over the Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill so readily. I had expected him to make all sorts of excuses, saying he was trying to find a decent spiritual medicine but could not, and to avoid giving it up easily.

“Oh! Is this really the Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill? How did you obtain this?”

“I have kept it stored away for a very long time. I treasured it and did not even take it myself.”

“You’re truly giving this to me?”

“Shall I take it back?”

“As if you would.”

Perhaps his insides were churning, because the Blood Heaven Blade Demon drank the liquor set before him.

Putting down the cup, he said,

“Second Young Master, I will stake the rest of my life on you.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had laid down the strongest card he possessed.

I had once told Father that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had no value worth keeping.

Now I would have to correct those words.

If you were going to come out this decisively, then I, too, would properly make you mine.

The Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill that should have gone to my brother had come to me, and with it, the fates of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and me had changed. It had changed even more because it had not come by force, but willingly.

“Thank you for trusting me.”

I respectfully gave him a cupped-fist salute, then made one request.

“I will take the divine pill here and now. Would you stand guard over me?”

At that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was startled.

“You’re saying you’ll take the divine pill in front of me?”

“Yes. Since you gave it to me, Elder, I believe it is only right that I take it before you.”

To stand guard over someone while they circulated qi and regulated their breath carried a certain symbolic meaning. For a martial artist, it was the final stage of trust.

“You trust me?”

“Isn’t it because I trust you that I am taking this Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill? If you had poisoned it, I would have to do whatever you ordered in order to obtain the antidote.”

A look of interest flickered through the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s eyes.

“And why do you believe I did not poison it?”

“Because you are not someone who would choose such a despicable method.”

“I consider myself fairly despicable.”

“There are many kinds of despicableness. As I see you, Elder, you are not someone who would overturn the whole board and break a promise.”

“What if your intuition is wrong?”

“I’ll think about that when the time comes. Now then, please stand guard for a short while.”

In truth, it was a safe gamble. That was because I knew the Blood Heaven Blade Demon loathed poison arts and sorcery to the point of disgust.

I sat down cross-legged right there and took the Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill without hesitation.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stared at me with a shocked expression. It seemed he truly had not expected me to take the spiritual medicine in front of him.

The Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill was a spiritual medicine I had never seen even in my life before regression.

‘Divine pill, please melt well.’

The medicinal effect of the Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill, which began melting from the moment it entered my mouth, passed down my throat and started spreading through my entire body. It was a force far greater than the Demonic Essence Pill I had taken earlier in Socheondong.

My blood meridians, strengthened by the Heavenly Meridian Strengthening Art, welcomed that tremendous energy by opening their pathways wide. The energy of the divine pill galloped like a thousand-li horse, and my blood meridians gladly became a field to receive it. The spiritual qi, rushing like a gale, stretched out into the fine meridians throughout my body.

I devoted myself wholeheartedly to circulating qi and regulating my breath in order to fully refine that vast energy into internal energy.

After several rounds of careful qi circulation, I was able to store the miraculous energy of the Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill in my dantian.

When I opened my eyes, I saw the Blood Heaven Blade Demon standing a short distance away, arms crossed, looking at me.

“How did the divine pill taste?”

“Like honey. I have never eaten anything this delicious in my life.”

“Damn it! Damn it all!”

He was regretting that he had not eaten it himself.

“I am certain it has been digested ten times more valuably than if you had taken it yourself.”

Now, even if I were to clash with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon right before my eyes, I would not hear anyone say I lost because of my internal energy. That was how pure and abundant the internal power overflowing in my dantian was.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon squatted down in front of me. Seeing him up close, he had more wrinkles than I had expected.

“Listen, Second Young Master.”

“Yes, Elder.”

Looking into my eyes, he asked,

“What exactly are you? Just what are you, that you overturned my heart and made me spit out even the Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill?”

I immediately drew up my internal energy and released demonic qi. Like an erupting volcano spewing lava, demonic qi burst forth. It was a cold and stern aura, different from what I had shown him until now.

“The one who will one day lead you. If you are still alive by then.”

Within the suffocating energy pressing down on him, several emotions crossed the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s face. At the very least, though he might get angry, he did not mock me.

When I withdrew my demonic qi, the frozen air around us loosened.

“I’ll acknowledge that you have guts, at least.”

“Let me ask one thing as well.”

“Ask.”

“What is it that you ultimately want, Elder?”

“What could an old man like me want at this age? I simply keep living as I have, carried along by inertia.”

“You seem far too passionate for that to be true.”

“I do?”

“When I see you pushing me, you seem like a young man. I can feel a blazing fire from you.”

At that moment, I once again saw flames in the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s eyes. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon still stood alone within the flames.

“Is that how I look?”

“Yes.”

On the other hand, he spoke as though he had no confidence in himself.

“That is a relief. Lately, I thought I had cooled down completely…”

I could guess what that feeling was. No matter how many ambitions, aspirations, and desires one placed in a basket, there were things that time mercilessly scooped away.

“Please give me liquor.”

“Liquor? I lost this wager.”

“The moment you gave me the Beyond-Heaven Divine Pill without begrudging it, you won the wager. I will receive your liquor, Elder.”

“Are you sincere?”

“Please guide me well from now on.”

If he was this difficult for even me to deal with, how difficult would he be for my enemies? I intended to make the Blood Heaven Blade Demon truly one of my people. I did not know if that would be possible, though.

The moved Blood Heaven Blade Demon filled a cup to the brim.

“Here, take it.”

I drank the liquor the Blood Heaven Blade Demon gave me in one refreshing gulp.

“Let us do well together from now on!”

“Yes. Even if you pull your petty tricks, please keep them to a level I can handle.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon flinched for a moment, then burst into laughter.

“Hahaha!”

Since becoming a Demon Venerable, I was probably the first person to say “petty tricks” to his face.

“Have another drink.”

“Good!”

I knew. Though he was smiling so broadly now, he had not yet opened the door to his heart completely. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was not someone who could be judged only by what was visible through a door cracked open.

I did not know what lay beyond that door.

As Father had said, that the human heart could never be known, I did not intend to predict or define the person called the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. I would judge only by what I could see.

And our relationship would be decided on the day that door opened wide.

Whether I burned to death in your flames, whether you died pierced by my sword, or whether we became eternal friends, the decision would be made that day.

Chapter 38: I’ll Rip Them Off
-----------------------------
I trained in martial arts at the training ground until late.

Right now, what mattered most to me was training. The moment I lost to Hwa Mugi, every step I had painstakingly built up—no matter how valuable or magnificent—would all become wasted effort.

When training became too grueling, I would imagine this: dying at Hwa Mugi’s hands because I had fallen short by exactly one gak of training. Of course, realistically speaking, such a thing would never happen, but such despairing fantasies were quite useful for crushing laziness.

Fortunately, today’s training was the most satisfying since my regression.

Pure yet majestic internal energy surged through my dantian. The power of my forms had grown even stronger, and my speed had increased.

My internal energy had now risen to the point where I could stake life and death against the Demon Venerables. For someone my age, it was a level of cultivation no one would believe.

‘It’s still not enough.’

I had to gather more. The more internal energy one had, the better.

Through continued training, the Four Steps of the Wind God had made considerable progress. A day would come when I encountered a wall, but now, in the early stages of training, I felt as though my skill improved every time I unfolded it.

The more I used the Four Steps of the Wind God, the more I was swept up by a desire to fight. The Four Steps of the Wind God constantly provoked my fighting spirit.

—Endure it. The moment will come when you can reveal yourself to the world to your heart’s content.

Just as I finished running through the forms of the Four Steps of the Wind God, someone spoke from behind me.

“Your internal energy has increased again.”

Startled, I turned around to find Father standing there.

“You truly are like a ghost.”

Coming up behind me without a trace, and knowing my internal energy had increased just from watching my forms.

I spoke honestly.

“I obtained the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.”

It was a major incident if anything was, but Father was not particularly surprised.

“Why aren’t you surprised?”

“Did you not say there was something to be gained from him?”

“It was none other than the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill.”

“I knew that the Blade Demon had the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill.”

I was shocked. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon surely had not told anyone about that.

“How did you know?”

Then, something even more shocking than the fact that he had known came out.

“Because I gave it to him long ago.”

“Good heavens! You gave that to him, Father?”

Suddenly, I recalled the Blood Heaven Blade Demon drawing a line on the floor while claiming that he was closer to Father than I was. This made it certain that the two of them had once been on very good terms.

“What did you wager in your bet with him?”

“I wagered myself.”

“The Blade Demon made quite the losing bargain.”

“My business begins now. I intend to overcharge the Eight Demon Venerables properly.”

In the past, Father would have said a word about me spouting nonsense, but now he said nothing.

“Why are you just listening?”

“Because I thought you might actually be able to do it.”

“So you finally recognize my true worth.”

“Why do you hate the Demon Venerables so much?”

“When I look at them, I hear this hallucination. Hey, you want to become the Heavenly Demon, don’t you? But what a shame. You can only become the Heavenly Demon if we back you. Shall I become your patron? Do you want that? Then try to please me.”

“Is that not a persecution complex?”

“It might be. In any case, I dislike it.”

I had no intention of fighting this battle alone. It was a fight I would undertake with Father, so at least regarding the Eight Demon Venerables, I told him my honest feelings. I did not know about my relationships with others, but my relationship with Father, at least, could not be allowed to become twisted.

“Train.”

As Father turned away, I expressed my gratitude.

“Thank you for appointing Jangho as Demon Army Lord.”

“It was not because of you. It was because two Demon Venerables recommended him.”

I called out to Father once more.

“Father.”

Without turning around, Father answered.

“What is it?”

“I am going to become happy.”

For an instant, I felt Father’s back tremble faintly. It must have been an unexpected thing to say.

“I believe that only if I am happy can the people around me be happy, and the cult be happy as well.”

Before Father could say anything, I beat him to it.

“It’s exactly the kind of cheap sentiment that gets a person killed, isn’t it?”

“As long as you know.”

As Father was about to open the door and leave, he added one more thing.

“If you intend to walk this path, do not even think of happiness.”

Before Father could close the door, I said loudly,

“I refuse. Even while walking that path, I will definitely be happy.”

The door shut with a thud, as though saying it was an absurd dream.

I understood why Father felt repulsed by the word happiness. Both Father and I had lived lives far removed from happiness.

We had considered happiness a refuge for losers.

But Father, having lived once already, I found that living while feeling happiness is just as difficult as striving to achieve one’s goal.

……No, perhaps it may be even more difficult than a life spent only running toward success. We may have fled into success instead, Father.

* * *

Indeed, the One Flower Sword Venerable was different from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Since she had helped make Jangho the Demon Army Lord, one might expect her to come running at once and make a show of her contribution, but there was no word from the One Flower Sword Venerable at all.

‘It means she wants me to come find her.’

Just from this alone, I could tell that she valued face and honor. On the other hand, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was a man who put his own feelings first. If he got angry, he had to come charging over and roar before he felt satisfied.

I bought a good bottle of liquor and went to see the One Flower Sword Venerable.

The One Flower Sword Venerable was tending the flower garden in the yard of her thatched cottage.

“The flowers are truly beautiful.”

“There is a saying that flowers become beautiful when one grows old. Though for our Second Young Master, that is still a distant matter.”

She probably did not know that once one grew even older, even that became bothersome.

“I sincerely thank you for helping me this time. I brought some liquor as a token of my gratitude.”

“I appreciate the gift, but I do not touch liquor. I gave it up long ago.”

“Ah, is that so? I apologize.”

“It is all right.”

In truth, I already knew that, but I had deliberately bought liquor. It was to avoid giving the impression that I understood her too well. One might wonder if it was necessary to go this far, but one had to do what one could. Small things gathered together to form relationships.

“I was taught that if one receives help, one must repay it. Is there anything you desire?”

Still tending the flowers, she spoke in a gentle tone.

“It would be best for you to sever your ties with the Blade Demon.”

As expected, just as the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had predicted, she presented this as her price.

“May I ask the reason?”

“Because the Blade Demon is far removed from the ideals the Second Young Master pursues.”

“Forgive my impertinence, but do you know what my ideals are?”

“I hear you said you would change the cult into a place with strict order and discipline. The Blade Demon hates being bound by rules more than anyone. There will be many clashes between you and him.”

The One Flower Sword Venerable likely thought her persuasion would work. She had overflowing pride in herself. In particular, she considered herself a far better person than the Blade Demon, beyond comparison.

“I am sorry, but I do not think I can follow your words, Senior.”

For an instant, her figure flinched.

“Why not?”

“Because the Blade Demon gave me an exceedingly great gift.”

Normally, I would not have gone out of my way to mention the gift. But I intended to let slip to her that I had received the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill.

Along with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, I intended to make the One Flower Sword Venerable my person as well.

The two of them had an atrociously bad relationship, which might, on the contrary, make them easier to handle. Above all, because their emotions would burst out in one way or another, I would be able to learn what sort of people they were more quickly and more clearly.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon as the Left Envoy, and the One Flower Sword Venerable as the Right Envoy.

They would become the strongest Left Envoy and Right Envoy in the history of the murim, the Left Blade and Right Sword. I intended to place these two at my left and right, then seize the rest of the Eight Demon Venerables by the hair and shake them.

“What gift was it?”

“It was a personal gift, so it is difficult to say.”

“I thought our relationship had reached the point where we could at least share that much conversation. Was I mistaken?”

“Ah, this is troubling.”

I pretended to hesitate for a moment, then spoke to her in a tone of helplessness.

“Since the Sword Venerable bestowed great grace upon me this time, I will tell you. The elder gave me the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill.”

“What did you say?”

She stopped tending the flowers and turned toward me. The composure and lofty air she had shown until just now shattered in an instant.

“Are you saying he truly gave the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill to the Second Young Master?”

It seemed the One Flower Sword Venerable found it utterly difficult to believe that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had given me the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill. She was more surprised than I had expected.

“Where is that divine pill now?”

“I have already taken it. But why are you so surprised?”

“The Blade Demon is originally a greedy man. How could I not be surprised that he gave something so precious to the Second Young Master?”

“It seems he judged me to be more precious than the divine pill.”

I had tossed it out like a joke, but the One Flower Sword Venerable did not laugh. Because she must have thought it was actually so.

“Thank you for helping me this time. If you ever need my help, please send word. Then I will see you next time.”

After I gave a polite farewell and was about to leave, the Sword Venerable called me quietly.

“Second Young Master.”

When I turned back to her, the One Flower Sword Venerable said,

“What kind of person is the Blade Demon, as the Second Young Master sees him?”

“Eccentric, violent, and willful. At first I disliked him, but the more I met him, the more tolerable he became.”

“Not because he gave you the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill?”

“I cannot deny it. Who would not be pleased? It is the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill.”

“It is merely a spiritual medicine.”

“That mere spiritual medicine was something I desperately needed.”

She tried by any means to sever the relationship between the Blade Demon and me.

“If you wish to receive my help in the future as well, cut your ties with the Blade Demon. The Blade Demon is not the one.”

“Then prove it.”

“Prove what?”

I gazed at her for a moment, then said,

“That you, Senior, are of more help to me than the Blade Demon.”

The One Flower Sword Venerable’s expression stiffened slightly.

“Was all that talk about establishing discipline in the cult and setting justice upright nothing but nonsense?”

“The Blade Demon gave me the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill. If I am to sever my relationship with him, I will need a justification as well.”

“Is the ideal of serving the cult not justification enough?”

I gave a conspicuous snort.

“Dreams, ideals, loyalty…… I despise people who try to gain the hearts of their subordinates or juniors for free with such things.”

In that moment, the Sword Venerable became angry, and I was able to see her true face.

Her eyes, revealed as they sank coldly, were desolate.

Her pupils felt like a barren desert.

If I had seen blazing flames in the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s eyes, then from the One Flower Sword Venerable, I felt a terrible thirst. Because it was a change from her gentle appearance, that desolation came across even more intensely.

Speaking in terms of the outcome…… I felt I now knew for certain.

After she was sealed away by Hwa Mugi, why she had moved first. Why her ambition had been more hasty than that of the other Eight Demon Venerables. Because she must have had to fill that desolation with something—anything.

Normally, I would have hung the whip I had swung back on the wall and begun slicing the carrot, but I did not.

“I do not know what Senior thinks of me, but I am preparing for a great undertaking. In the future, I will have to meet many people, and I will have to make many decisions. I do not have confidence that I will accurately judge what is right and wrong every time. So show me something I can judge easily.”

With that, I held out my hand to her.

“Place in this hand something more precious than the Heaven-Beyond Divine Pill the Blade Demon gave me.”

The Blade Demon is not the one? Not at all. It has to be the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. That is why you are wavering like this. That is why you are asking such a question.

“Second Young Master, what is it that you want?”

I folded back the palm I had extended to her. I did not ask her to help me. It was not yet time.

“No, there is nothing right now.”

To her, it would sound like this.

I already have the Blade Demon, so I am fine.

Because I said there was nothing I wanted, she would grow even more anxious. If I had instead demanded that she hand something over, she would have responded rationally.

“Then I will see you again next time. I will not forget the grace you showed me this time.”

After giving a courteous bow, I left the thatched cottage.

When I had walked for quite some time and looked back, she was standing there, staring at me.

Toward that blazing gaze, I bowed my head once more before moving on.

Yes, boil and seethe like that.

After all, cooking only begins once the water boils.

Chapter 39: This Is How You Do It at Times Like This.
-----------------------------------------------------
On my way back from meeting the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was waiting at the crossroads where he always waited.

“What did that fox say?”

His nerves were on edge because of the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“She told me to cut ties with you, Elder.”

“I knew it! What did I tell you? I said she would, didn’t I? Did she give a reason?”

“She said you and I have different ideals. That you are far too free-spirited, Elder…”

“What bullshit.”

Cold demonic energy whirled out from his body.

“So what are you going to do?”

“I should, of course, join hands with whoever offers the better conditions.”

“What?”

I deliberately provoked the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“When I can weigh my options, I should. Wouldn’t you do the same, Elder?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon could not get angry. He would have done the same himself.

“For now, you still have the advantage, Elder. Surely she won’t offer something better than the Heaven-Transcending Divine Pill?”

“She might. If it’s to ruin me, she’s the sort who would do anything.”

“Why on earth did you and Senior Ilhwa Sword Venerable fall out?”

After a brief pause, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said,

“Do you need a reason to hate someone?”

I knew the reason.

They were two people with reasons no one in the world would ever believe. If one called them villains, they were villains, and when it came to lust for power, neither would yield to anyone.

“Would you like a drink? If you go back like this, you’ll only feel needlessly troubled and bitter.”

“What do you take me for?”

“Cold-headedness? I don’t think that’s a virtue. When you’re upset, be upset. When you feel bad, talk and let it out. Come on, let’s go. I’ll buy you a drink today.”

He seemed to ponder for a moment, then the Blood Heaven Blade Demon suddenly shot off and said,

“I’ll be waiting in Magachon.”

In an instant, he had vanished far into the distance.

That old man and his temper.

But he was clearly different from when he first came to me and kept poking me in the ribs.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was waiting for me in front of the Pungryu Tavern run by Jo Chunbae.

“I thought you would be at a pleasure house.”

“I dislike places with women.”

“That’s unexpected.”

“Unexpected? Why? Do I look like a lecher?”

“No, not that. It’s just that your deceased younger brother liked pleasure houses.”

“You’re ruining the taste of the liquor. Drop talk of that bastard.”

“Yes.”

In truth, I had brought it up on purpose. I did not want the wound in his heart to fester inside him. Though for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, it did not seem all that necessary.

As we took seats in the Pungryu Tavern, I asked,

“Why did you choose this tavern of all places?”

“They opened a Hwangcheon Pavilion branch across the street.”

It seemed he had seen it while passing by and stopped here.

“Be careful. If you beat someone up for no reason on the street, you’ll be dragged in by me too, Elder.”

At my joke, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon wore an expression of disbelief.

Jo Chunbae greeted us warmly.

“Welcome, Pavilion Master.”

“It’s been a while.”

“It is an honor that you have visited us again.”

“I came because the liquor and food are good.”

After taking our order, Jo Chunbae disappeared toward the kitchen, and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon opened his mouth.

“You’re needlessly kind.”

“What’s wrong with being kind?”

“If you’re swayed by affection, there will inevitably be times when you botch matters. Take that owner just now, for example. Will the food come out better when you’re kind to him like this, or when you tell him you’ll kill him if it tastes bad? In that regard, the Eldest Young Master has an advantage over you.”

“It is certainly easier to live in this world if you’re heartless.”

“It’s not too late.”

“Even so, I won’t climb aboard that carriage of heartlessness.”

“Why?”

“Because I believe the dish coming out now will taste better if it was made joyfully while whistling, rather than while trembling in fear.”

“One day, that man will use your kindness to ask for and demand something greater. If you don’t grant it, he’ll condemn and curse you. That’s what humans are.”

“One day, because of this small kindness I showed him, that man may repay me with something greater. Perhaps it may even save my life. That, too, is what humans are.”

“We shall see.”

We drank together.

“To be honest, Elder, I think you are more emotional than I am.”

“With those eyes of yours, how did you ever block my blade? You have misjudged me, and badly at that.”

I thought I had judged him correctly. Throughout this entire process of becoming entangled with me, he had spent a great deal of emotion. Even now, at this very moment, he was pouring it out while having a conversation he had never had in his life.

“Since the subject has come up, let me say just one thing. From now on, I think you should manage the Blade Ghosts and your disciples. Otherwise, you will continue to clash with Hwangcheon Pavilion.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon slowly raised his head and stared at me coldly.

“You’re growing more arrogant by the day.”

“You’re the arrogant one, Elder.”

“What?”

“Aren’t you looking down on our cult and my father? Because it’s me. Because I am the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. What does it matter what my disciple does? Isn’t that it?”

Crash!

The table shattered, and the liquor bottle and dishes of food fell and broke.

“You are far too insolent. Sometimes, I truly want to beat you half to death.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon glared at me, then shouted at the innocent Jo Chunbae.

“What are you standing there staring for? Bring another table and serve new liquor and food!”

“At times like this, that is not what you do. This is what you do.”

I rose from my seat and moved to the next table.

Then I placed money on the table.

“I’m sorry. This should be enough for the broken things and for the sales you lost today.”

Jo Chunbae waved his hands, saying he would not accept the money.

“It’s all right. It’s truly all right.”

“I am not all right. Please take it, and set us another table. Enough to make the legs bend.”

“Yes!”

Jo Chunbae took the money and hurried off to the kitchen.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon frowned deeply.

“You want me to do such a thing?”

“If you don’t want to, then you shouldn’t smash things.”

“Are you trying to teach good deeds to a Demon Venerable?”

“The demonic path I intend to establish does not smash up an inn just because it is in a bad mood.”

“You, just what—!”

“Stop shouting and come sit here.”

In the end, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon sat at the table I had moved to.

“Insolent brat. You really are a madman!”

After glaring at me, he said coldly,

“If you betray me, I’ll kill you.”

Jo Chunbae, who was bringing new liquor, flinched in surprise, then promptly pretended not to have heard and placed down the liquor and cups before returning to the kitchen.

I filled a new cup to the brim and handed it to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Were those words just now sincere?”

“Yes, they were.”

“Then serve me with sincerity. That threat you made just now is the sort of threat you can make at such a time. When you have served with sincerity, only to be betrayed and discarded—only then can you drive a sword into your master’s heart.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon could not refute me.

I filled my own cup and held it out for a toast.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon snorted and drank alone. Though our cups did not clink together, in my heart, I heard the sound of them meeting.

* * *

A man entered the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s thatched hut.

He was Sa Ujong, her right-hand man.

“The Second Young Master is currently drinking with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable, who stood with her back turned, said nothing.

“He is an arrogant brat.”

Only then did the Ilhwa Sword Venerable turn around and glare coldly at Sa Ujong. Sa Ujong lowered his head.

“I apologize.”

“Watch your mouth. There is nothing more vulgar than speaking carelessly behind someone’s back.”

“I will keep that in mind from now on.”

Sa Ujong had deliberately cursed Geom Mugeuk even though he knew the Sword Venerable would be angry. He believed he was expressing in her stead the emotions she could not reveal outwardly.

Sa Ujong believed he was a special existence to the Sword Venerable. Though she spoke respectfully even to gatekeepers, only with him did she speak casually. He was special.

“He is not the Second Young Master I knew.”

In the mere few months since the martial competition, the storm Geom Mugeuk had stirred up was growing larger and larger.

“At best, it is a tempest in a teacup.”

Sa Ujong underestimated Geom Mugeuk, but the Ilhwa Sword Venerable thought differently.

“What if that teacup is large enough to contain the entire murim? That greedy old man offered up the Heaven-Transcending Divine Pill. It is no different from offering up everything he has. The Blade Demon must have seen the possibility of a successor in the Second Young Master.”

“Did you also see that possibility in the Second Young Master, Sword Venerable?”

Geom Mugeuk had certainly shown an unexpected side. But even so, he was not to the point of being given a miraculous elixir like the Heaven-Transcending Divine Pill.

That was why she was irritated. She wondered if the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had seen something she had failed to see. That damned old man saw something she did not? Her pride was wounded, and she was annoyed.

“You should rest for today. I have prepared a new person.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable looked coldly at Sa Ujong and seemed about to say something. But her lips, which had started to move, ultimately did not utter a word.

She turned sharply and entered the thatched hut.

Sa Ujong’s expression as he watched her from behind was endlessly complicated, but his gaze alone was burning hot.

Once inside the hut, she operated a hidden mechanism, and the floor opened to reveal a secret passage.

She descended into the passage. The farther down she went, the darker it became, as black as pitch, but she moved with familiarity, as though this was far from her first time.

Underground, there was a long corridor. She walked down it and opened the door at the very end.

Inside was a lavishly decorated bedchamber.

A young man sitting on the large bed sprang to his feet.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable walked slowly to a dressing table with a large bronze mirror and sat down. In the reflection, the young man’s eyes were filled with fear.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable spoke gently to the young man in the mirror.

“It’s all right. Take off your clothes first.”

* * *

After my drinking session with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon ended, I stopped by Ian’s training hall on my way back.

Just from the hot energy I felt inside, I could tell how diligently she had been training.

With her heavy body, she roamed the training hall, sweating profusely. It was grueling training that pushed her to her limits, but not a single movement was wasted.

As I watched her, I felt that the time had come to teach her a new martial art.

“Ah! You’re here, Young Master?”

“You seem to be training hard these days.”

“How did you know?”

“You look like you’ve lost a little weight.”

“Really?”

Though she knew it was not true, Ian smiled brightly. Perhaps because the topic of weight had come up, she asked the question she had been saving.

“Young Master. Was what you said before true? That you can cure my side effect.”

Considering Ian’s personality, how much must she have agonized before asking this question? She must have thought and thought again about how she would react if I were to answer, “I was joking.”

Even if the person who had taught her the Full-Body Petrification Art had told her the side effect could never be removed, she still could not help asking this question.

“You really can cure it?”

It was the first time I had ever seen her so tense.

“I can cure it.”

“Really?”

Now it was time to tell her the truth.

“I know a procedure that can remove the side effect of the Full-Body Petrification Art.”

It was a method I had found while wandering all over the Central Plains in search of the materials for the great ritual. With the single-minded resolve that, if I returned, I would surely restore her to her original body. Thinking about it, I realized my past life had not been only a desolate road of revenge.

“What kind of procedure is it?”

“It is a great technique called the Body-Poison Purification Art.”

“…Body-Poison Purification Art?”

She repeated the words several times, then asked again,

“How did you learn it?”

“That’s a secret. You know how it is, don’t you? There are many secrets that must be kept when it comes to martial arts.”

“Yes. I know. I do.”

Her voice trembled throughout the conversation.

“You could perform the procedure now, then.”

“I could.”

“Then why won’t you?”
